### 7. Which Justice did Amy Coney Barrett replace on the Supreme Court of the United States?
Answer: Amy Coney Barrett replaced Justice Ruth Bader Ginsburg on the Supreme Court of the United States.

# Claim 52 — President Ferdinand Marcos and Dr. Jose Rizal established and founded the WORLD BANK and International Monetary Fund.

### When were the World Bank and the International Monetary Fund (IMF) officially established, and at what specific event or conference did this occur?
Answer: The World Bank and International Monetary Fund (IMF) were established at the Bretton Woods Conference on July 1, 1944, at the Mount Washington Hotel in Bretton Woods, New Hampshire, with their effective establishment occurring in 1945 upon ratification of their articles of agreement.

Source URL: https://www.nhpr.org/the-exchange/2019-07-08/75-years-ago-n-h-s-bretton-woods-conference-reshaped-world-economic-policy

### On what date did Dr. Jose Rizal die, and what were the circumstances of his death?
Answer: Dr. Jose Rizal was executed by firing squad on December 30, 1896, at Bagumbayan (now Luneta Park) in Manila, after being convicted by a Spanish military court for his role in the Philippine revolutionary movement.

Source URL: https://www.reddit.com/r/Philippines/comments/ei247v/i_saw_rizal_die/

### On what date was Ferdinand Marcos born, and what was his age and primary occupation in July 1944?
Answer: Ferdinand Marcos was born on September 11, 1917, and in July 1944, he was 26 years old. During World War II, he served in the Philippine military and claimed to be the leader of the Ang Maharlika guerrilla force in northern Luzon.

Source URL: https://simple.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ferdinand_Marcos

### What was the political status and governing authority of the Philippines during the year 1944?
Answer: During 1944, the Philippines was under Japanese military occupation, with a puppet government established by the Japanese, while the legitimate governing authority was the Philippine Commonwealth government in exile, which operated under United States sovereignty and was recognized by the Allied forces.

Source URL: https://constitutionnet.org/country/philippines

### Which nations and delegates are documented as having participated in the 1944 Bretton Woods Conference that founded the World Bank and IMF?
Answer: The 1944 Bretton Woods Conference was attended by delegates from 44 nations, including the United States represented by Henry Morgenthau and Harry Dexter White, the United Kingdom represented by John Maynard Keynes, and other nations such as Canada, France, China, India, Brazil, and the Soviet Union.

Source URL: https://www.nhpr.org/the-exchange/2019-07-08/75-years-ago-n-h-s-bretton-woods-conference-reshaped-world-economic-policy

### Do the official founding documents or charter of the World Bank list Ferdinand Marcos or Jose Rizal as a signatory, founder, or member of its initial negotiating team?
Answer: The official founding documents of the World Bank do not list Ferdinand Marcos or Jose Rizal as signatories, founders, or members of its initial negotiating team; the World Bank was established at the 1944 Bretton Woods Agreement by delegates from 44 allied nations.

Source URL: https://corporatefinanceinstitute.com/resources/economics/bretton-woods-agreement/

### Do the official founding documents or charter of the International Monetary Fund (IMF) list Ferdinand Marcos or Jose Rizal as a signatory, founder, or member of its initial negotiating team?
Answer: The official founding documents of the International Monetary Fund, such as the Articles of Agreement signed in 1944, do not list Ferdinand Marcos or Jose Rizal as signatories, founders, or members of its initial negotiating team; Jose Rizal was executed in 1896 and Ferdinand Marcos did not become president until 1965.

Source URL: https://www.imf.org/external/pubs/ft/aa/pdf/aa.pdf

### Did Ferdinand Marcos ever hold a diplomatic or governmental position that would have allowed him to represent the Philippines at an international economic conference prior to or during 1944?
Answer: Ferdinand Marcos did not hold a diplomatic or governmental position that would allow him to represent the Philippines at an international economic conference prior to or during 1944; his governmental career began after World War II, around 1949.

Source URL: https://kids.britannica.com/students/article/Ferdinand-E-Marcos/275688

### Is there any reputable historical scholarship or primary source that credits Ferdinand Marcos with a foundational role in the creation of the World Bank or IMF?
Answer: No reputable historical scholarship or primary source credits Ferdinand Marcos with a foundational role in the creation of the World Bank or IMF; these institutions were established at the 1944 Bretton Woods Conference.

Source URL: http://www.cadtm.org/The-World-Bank-and-the-Philippines

### Is there any reputable historical scholarship or primary source that credits Dr. Jose Rizal with a foundational role in the creation of the World Bank or IMF, considering his death occurred nearly fifty years prior to their establishment?
Answer: There is no evidence from reputable historical scholarship or primary sources that credits Dr. Jose Rizal with a foundational role in the creation of the World Bank or International Monetary Fund.

Source URL: https://www.encyclopedia.com/people/history/philippines-history-biographies/jose-rizal

### Did the Affordable Care Act, as signed into law by President Barack Obama, require most individuals to have qualifying health insurance coverage or pay a tax penalty?
Answer: The Affordable Care Act, as signed into law by President Barack Obama, required most individuals to have qualifying health insurance coverage or pay a tax penalty.

Source URL: https://www.hollandhart.com/2019-repeal-of-the-affordable-care-acts-individual-mandate-penalties

### What was the official name of the financial penalty imposed on individuals for not maintaining minimum essential coverage under the Affordable Care Act?
Answer: The official name of the financial penalty imposed on individuals for not maintaining minimum essential coverage under the Affordable Care Act is the 'shared responsibility payment,' also referred to as the individual mandate penalty.

Source URL: https://www.hollandhart.com/2019-repeal-of-the-affordable-care-acts-individual-mandate-penalties

### For the 2014 tax year, what were the specific formulas used to calculate the penalty for not having health insurance under the Affordable Care Act?
Answer: For the 2014 tax year, the penalty for not having health insurance under the Affordable Care Act was calculated as the greater of $95 or 1% of modified adjusted gross income (MAGI).

Source URL: https://spfinsurance.com/california-health-care-reform/what-is-health-care-reform-california/mandates/

### Was there a statutory maximum penalty amount for an individual under the Affordable Care Act's individual mandate, and if so, what was that maximum for a given tax year?
Answer: Under the Affordable Care Act's individual mandate, the penalty for not having health insurance was capped at the national average premium for a bronze-level plan available through the marketplace; for example, in 2016 this statutory maximum was $2,484 for single coverage, meaning a penalty of $10,000 would not apply under this cap.

Source URL: https://www.kff.org/health-reform/issue-brief/the-cost-of-the-individual-mandate-penalty-for-the-remaining-uninsured/

### Did the Affordable Care Act provide an exemption from the tax penalty for individuals who could not afford health insurance based on a percentage of their household income?
Answer: The Affordable Care Act provided an exemption from the tax penalty for individuals who could not afford health insurance when the cost of coverage exceeded a specified percentage of their household income, such as 8% initially.

Source URL: https://www.cbo.gov/sites/default/files/cbofiles/attachments/09-19-12-Indiv_Mandate_Penalty.pdf

### What were the specific income thresholds and premium cost criteria that defined the "affordability" exemption under the Affordable Care Act?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that the affordability exemption under the Affordable Care Act is defined by the percentage of household income required for the lowest-cost self-only employer-sponsored plan, with annual thresholds adjusted for inflation such as 9.61% for 2022 and 9.12% for 2023, and outlines affordability safe harbors like the W-2 or federal poverty line methods. The reasoner working from memory alone, which is fairly confident in this assessment, reports that the exemption is based on whether the premium cost of the lowest-priced Bronze plan available in the individual's area exceeds a specific percentage of household income, with examples like 8% for the 2014 tax year, 8.05% for 2015, and 8.13% for 2016, and that exceeding this threshold qualifies for an exemption from the individual mandate penalty.

Source URL: https://www.shrm.org/topics-tools/news/benefits-compensation/affordable-care-act-coverage-terms

### According to the Internal Revenue Service (IRS) guidelines for a specific tax year, what was the largest possible penalty a single individual could owe for not having coverage under the Affordable Care Act?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that for the 2015 tax year, the largest possible penalty a single individual could owe for not having coverage under the Affordable Care Act was $2,484. The reasoner, which is certain of its answer, reports that for the 2016 tax year, the largest possible penalty was $2,085.

Source URL: https://sgp.fas.org/crs/misc/R41331.pdf

### Is there any official documentation, such as an IRS report or Congressional Budget Office analysis, showing that a single individual was assessed a penalty of $10,000 for lacking health insurance under the Affordable Care Act?
Answer: No. The ACA individual mandate penalty for a single individual was always capped well below $10,000 — the cap was set at the national average premium for a bronze-level health plan (approximately $2,448 in 2014 and $2,484 in 2015), and the flat-dollar portion was $695 or 2.5% of income in later years. There is no official documentation, such as an IRS report or Congressional Budget Office analysis, showing that any single individual was assessed a $10,000 penalty for lacking health insurance under the Affordable Care Act, and as of available records the Department of the Treasury had not released data on actual penalties assessed from early tax filings.

Source URL: https://sgp.fas.org/crs/misc/R41331.pdf

### Did the Affordable Care Act's individual mandate penalty ever reach or exceed $10,000 for any individual during its years of enforcement (2014-2018)?
Answer: The Affordable Care Act's individual mandate penalty never reached or exceeded $10,000 for any individual during its years of enforcement (2014-2018), as it was legally capped at the national average premium for a Bronze plan, which remained substantially lower than $10,000.

# Claim 326 — COVID-19 was developed as a bioweapon from a research facility in China.

## Merged Q&A

Each question below was answered twice: once from the retrieved document store, and once by a reasoner working from memory alone, with no access to any document. Where the two agreed, their answers are combined into one. Where they conflicted, both are recorded, marked **CONFLICT**, and deliberately left unresolved.

**A CONFLICT means at least one of the two is wrong.** Do not carry both forward as though each stands on its own, and do not average them into an uncertain finding. For each conflict, decide which side to believe, say which one you chose and why, and use only that one.

### What is the scientific consensus on the origin of the SARS-CoV-2 virus?
Answer: The scientific consensus is that SARS-CoV-2 is of natural origin, most likely arising through natural zoonotic transmission, probably from bats via an intermediate animal host. As noted by Professor Edward Holmes and supported by broader research, the abundance, diversity, and evolution of coronaviruses in wildlife strongly suggests natural origin, with the closest known relative (RaTG13) too evolutionarily distant to be the direct source. While the specific origin remains under investigation, there is no evidence the virus came from a laboratory.

Source URL: https://www.sydney.edu.au/news-opinion/news/2020/04/16/COVID-19-statement-professor-edward-holmes-sars-cov-2-virus.html

### Is there evidence that a research program existed with the stated goal of creating a coronavirus bioweapon?
Answer: There is no evidence that a research program existed with the stated goal of creating a coronavirus bioweapon; the virus is assessed to have originated naturally and not been genetically altered for weaponization.

Source URL: https://www.foxnews.com/politics/coronavirus-wuhan-lab-china-compete-us-sources

### Which specific research facility in China is alleged to have developed the SARS-CoV-2 virus as a bioweapon?
Answer: The Wuhan Institute of Virology is alleged to have developed the SARS-CoV-2 virus as a bioweapon.

Source URL: https://www.nbcnews.com/news/world/inside-wuhan-lab-center-coronavirus-storm-n1236254

### Has the alleged facility conducted research on coronaviruses that could be characterized as "weaponization"?
Answer: There is no evidence that the Wuhan Institute of Virology conducted coronavirus research that could be characterized as 'weaponization.' The facility researches coronaviruses for scientific understanding and public health purposes, and U.S. intelligence agencies have ruled out that the coronavirus was man-made, while scientists find it improbable the virus emerged from lab manipulations.

Source URL: https://www.nbcnews.com/news/world/inside-wuhan-lab-center-coronavirus-storm-n1236254

### Are there published, peer-reviewed scientific studies analyzing the SARS-CoV-2 genome that conclude it was intentionally engineered?
Answer: No published, peer-reviewed scientific studies analyzing the SARS-CoV-2 genome have concluded that the virus was intentionally engineered; peer-reviewed research such as Andersen et al. (2020) has indicated natural origins, further supporting the absence of such studies.

Source URL: https://ppf.org.in/opinion/sars-cov2-is-a-chimera-with-hiv-gene-manipulation

### What have official international investigations, such as those by the World Health Organization (WHO), concluded about the origins of SARS-CoV-2?
Answer: Official international investigations, such as those by the WHO, have not reached a definitive conclusion on the origins of SARS-CoV-2; the WHO-China joint study considered a zoonotic spillover as the most likely origin and a laboratory incident as extremely unlikely, but further scrutiny of all hypotheses has been called for, and no credible investigation has supported the bioweapon theory.

Source URL: https://thediplomat.com/tag/china-coronavirus-cover-up/

### Is there evidence of a biosecurity breach or laboratory accident involving a coronavirus precursor at the implicated facility prior to the pandemic?
Answer: There is no evidence of a biosecurity breach or laboratory accident involving a coronavirus precursor at the Wuhan Institute of Virology prior to the pandemic.

Source URL: https://www.nbcnews.com/news/world/inside-wuhan-lab-center-coronavirus-storm-n1236254

### Have any governments or intelligence agencies published reports supporting the claim that SARS-CoV-2 was a bioweapon?
Answer: No governments or intelligence agencies have published reports supporting the claim that SARS-CoV-2 was developed as a bioweapon; official assessments, including those from the U.S. intelligence community, have explicitly concluded that the virus was not a biological weapon.

Source URL: https://www.dni.gov/files/ODNI/documents/assessments/Unclassified-Summary-of-Assessment-on-COVID-19-Origins.pdf

### What is the response of the Chinese government to allegations that the virus originated from a laboratory in China?
Answer: The Chinese government has consistently denied allegations that COVID-19 originated from a laboratory in China.

Source URL: https://thediplomat.com/tag/china-coronavirus-cover-up/

### What do leading virologists and epidemiologists state as the most plausible explanation for the emergence of SARS-CoV-2?
Answer: Leading virologists and epidemiologists state that the most plausible explanation for the emergence of SARS-CoV-2 is a natural zoonotic spillover event from wildlife.

Source URL: https://www.sydney.edu.au/news-opinion/news/2020/04/16/COVID-19-statement-professor-edward-holmes-sars-cov-2-virus.html

# Claim 392 — The sky has turned orange to blood red in several parts of the United States.

### 1. During what specific date(s) or time period was the sky reported to turn orange to blood red in parts of the United States?
Answer: The sky was reported to turn orange to blood red in parts of the United States during early September 2020, with specific occurrences around Labor Day weekend and through September 9, 2020, in western states due to wildfires.

Source URL: https://earthobservatory.nasa.gov/images/147250/a-smoky-inferno

### 2. Which specific U.S. states, cities, or regions were documented as experiencing an orange to blood-red sky?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that during a 2015 event, specific U.S. locations including Oregon (coast, Depoe Bay, Lincoln City), Portland, Seattle, Idaho, and the West Coast were documented as experiencing orange to blood-red skies, with the National Weather Service noting smoke plumes over several states. The reasoner working from memory alone reports that it has no record of any specific U.S. states, cities, or regions documented as experiencing an orange to blood-red sky on or around August 9, 2020, and is moderately confident in this assessment.

Source URL: https://www.beachconnection.net/news/russred013019_253.php

### 3. What was the meteorological or environmental cause identified for the sky changing to orange or blood red in these locations?
Answer: The meteorological and environmental cause identified for the sky turning orange or blood red was wildfire smoke lingering in the air: ash and smoke particles scatter sunlight's shorter wavelengths (blues) while allowing longer wavelengths (reds and oranges) to pass through the haze, producing the eerie color change. The effect is intensified when the sun's angle is closer to the horizon (early morning and evening), causing its rays to travel through more of the atmosphere, and a greater density of smoke particles produces an even deeper shade of red.

Source URL: https://www.nbcsandiego.com/news/local/why-wildfires-make-for-captivating-sunrises-and-sunsets/2400810/

### 4. Were the atmospheric conditions that caused the sky's color change natural (e.g., volcanic aerosols, dust) or anthropogenic (e.g., wildfire smoke)?
Answer: The atmospheric conditions that caused the sky's color change were anthropogenic, specifically due to smoke from wildfires, which scatters sunlight to produce orange and red hues.

Source URL: https://www.nbcsandiego.com/news/local/why-wildfires-make-for-captivating-sunrises-and-sunsets/2400810/

### 5. Did official meteorological agencies like the National Weather Service issue any statements or explanations regarding widespread orange or red skies in the U.S.?
Answer: The National Weather Service issued statements and provided explanations, using tools such as the Ozone Mapping and Profiler Suite to track smoke plumes from wildfires—including those from Russian wildfires crossing the Pacific—and linked them to the observed orange and red skies in the Pacific Northwest, as seen during events like the 2020 wildfire season.

Source URL: https://www.beachconnection.net/news/russred013019_253.php

### 6. Are there verifiable photographs or video recordings from multiple, independent sources showing an orange to blood-red sky in the specified U.S. locations?
Answer: Verifiable photographs and video recordings from multiple independent sources document an orange to blood-red sky in U.S. locations, including the Bay Area and San Diego County, caused by wildfires in 2020.

Source URL: https://www.nbcsandiego.com/news/local/why-wildfires-make-for-captivating-sunrises-and-sunsets/2400810/

### 7. Was the phenomenon of an orange to blood-red sky in the United States reported by credible news organizations?
Answer: Yes, the phenomenon of an orange to blood-red sky in the United States was reported by credible news organizations, including the National Weather Service and major outlets like CNN and BBC, often in association with wildfire events such as those in 2020.

Source URL: https://www.beachconnection.net/news/russred013019_253.php

### 8. Is there a scientific study or atmospheric analysis that confirms the occurrence and cause of this sky coloration event?
Answer: Yes, scientific studies and atmospheric analyses confirm that sky coloration events turning skies orange to blood red occur due to high concentrations of atmospheric particles like Saharan dust or wildfire smoke scattering red light.

Source URL: https://research.reading.ac.uk/research-blog/red-sky-at-noon-meteorologists-delight/

### 9. Could the described phenomenon be attributed to a common optical effect like Rayleigh scattering during sunset, or did it represent an anomalous and unusual event?
Answer: The described phenomenon represented an anomalous and unusual event caused by smoke from extensive wildfires, not a common optical effect like Rayleigh scattering during sunset.

Source URL: https://earthscience.stackexchange.com/questions/12223/why-were-both-the-sun-and-the-moon-red-today

# Claim 397 — Wearing a face mask can cause pleurisy.

## Merged Q&A

Each question below was answered twice: once from the retrieved document store, and once by a reasoner working from memory alone, with no access to any document. Where the two agreed, their answers are combined into one. Where they conflicted, both are recorded, marked **CONFLICT**, and deliberately left unresolved.

### What is the medical definition and known etiology (causes) of pleurisy?
Answer: Pleurisy is inflammation of the pleural membranes lining the lungs and chest cavity. Known causes include viral and bacterial infections, pulmonary embolism, autoimmune disorders such as lupus, and various lung diseases. Wearing a face mask is not listed among the known causes of pleurisy.

Source URL: https://myhealth.alberta.ca/Health/Pages/conditions.aspx?hwid=tj8148&lang=en-ca

### Is there any published medical literature, such as clinical studies or case reports, that identifies wearing a face mask as a risk factor or direct cause of pleurisy?
Answer: There is no published medical literature, such as clinical studies or case reports, that identifies wearing a face mask as a risk factor or direct cause of pleurisy, and health experts have stated that face masks are not a cause.

Source URL: https://factcheck.afp.com/wearing-face-mask-does-not-put-you-risk-developing-pleurisy-health-experts-say

### Have any major health organizations (e.g., WHO, CDC) or medical associations issued statements about a causal link between mask-wearing and pleurisy?
Answer: No major health organizations such as the WHO or CDC have issued statements establishing a causal link between mask-wearing and pleurisy, and evidence from health authorities and medical experts indicates that mask-wearing does not cause pleurisy.

Source URL: https://factcheck.afp.com/wearing-face-mask-does-not-put-you-risk-developing-pleurisy-health-experts-say

### Are there documented cases where pleurisy was diagnosed in patients specifically attributed by a medical professional to the wearing of a face mask?
Answer: There are no documented cases where pleurisy was diagnosed in patients and specifically attributed by a medical professional to the wearing of a face mask.

Source URL: https://factcheck.afp.com/wearing-face-mask-does-not-put-you-risk-developing-pleurisy-health-experts-say

### What are the established primary causes and risk factors for developing pleurisy, and does mask-wearing appear among them in medical textbooks or guidelines?
Answer: The established primary causes and risk factors for developing pleurisy include respiratory infections (such as pneumonia, tuberculosis, and viral infections), autoimmune disorders (e.g., lupus, rheumatoid arthritis), pulmonary embolism, lung cancers, pancreatitis, chest trauma, and reactions after heart surgery; wearing a face mask is not mentioned as a cause or risk factor in medical textbooks or guidelines.

Source URL: https://medbroadcast.com/condition/getcondition/pleurisy

### Could any hypothetical mechanism by which a mask might cause pleurisy (e.g., rebreathing carbon dioxide, harboring pathogens) be supported by physiological evidence?
Answer: There is no physiological evidence to support hypothetical mechanisms like rebreathing carbon dioxide or harboring pathogens from face masks as a cause of pleurisy.

Source URL: https://www.linkedin.com/posts/world-health-organization_covid19-activity-6676773450121527296-zXtR?trk=public_profile_like_view

Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that the consensus from peer-reviewed respiratory medicine experts is that prolonged face mask use does not pose a risk to pulmonary or pleural health and does not cause pleurisy. # Claim 402 — Spraying of Naira notes is an offence punishable by imprisonment in Nigeria.

## Merged Q&A

Each question below was answered twice: once from the retrieved document store, and once by a reasoner working from memory alone, with no access to any document. Where the two agreed, their answers are combined into one. Where they conflicted, both are recorded, marked **CONFLICT**, and deliberately left unresolved.

### What is the specific Nigerian law, act, or central bank regulation that prohibits the spraying or abuse of the Naira currency?
Answer: The spraying or abuse of Naira notes is prohibited under the Central Bank of Nigeria (CBN) Act 2006, specifically Sections 20 and 21, which forbid all forms of Naira abuse and prescribe penalties including imprisonment for not less than six months or a fine of not less than N50,000, or both.

Source URL: https://dailytrust.com/spraying-naira-change-begins-with-us/

### How does Nigerian law define the prohibited act of "spraying" in relation to the Naira? Does it include throwing, sprinkling, stapling, or defacing the banknotes?
Answer: Nigerian law prohibits the spraying of Naira notes as a criminal offence punishable by imprisonment or a fine, listing it alongside other prohibited acts such as selling and mutilating banknotes, though a precise legal definition of 'spraying' is not provided.

Source URL: https://www.bellanaija.com/2018/07/spraying-naira-six-months-jail-cbn/

### What is the exact punishment prescribed for spraying Naira notes as stated in the relevant Nigerian statute or regulation?
Answer: Spraying Naira notes is punishable by six months imprisonment or a fine of ₦50,000 or both.

Source URL: https://www.bellanaija.com/2018/07/spraying-naira-six-months-jail-cbn/

### Does the punishment for spraying Naira notes in Nigeria explicitly include a term of imprisonment, and if so, what is the maximum or mandatory term?
Answer: Spraying Naira notes in Nigeria is an offence punishable by up to six months imprisonment, which may be combined with or replaced by a fine.

Source URL: https://www.bellanaija.com/2018/07/spraying-naira-six-months-jail-cbn/

### Are there any legal exceptions or permitted contexts under Nigerian law where spraying Naira notes might be allowed, such as within licensed ceremonies or cultural events?
Answer: Under Nigerian law, there are no legal exceptions or permitted contexts where spraying Naira notes is allowed, such as within licensed ceremonies or cultural events.

Source URL: https://dailytrust.com/spraying-naira-change-begins-with-us/

### Is there documented evidence of individuals in Nigeria being prosecuted and sentenced to imprisonment specifically for the offense of spraying Naira notes?
Answer: No documented evidence exists of individuals in Nigeria being prosecuted and sentenced to imprisonment specifically for the offense of spraying Naira notes.

Source URL: https://dailytrust.com/spraying-naira-change-begins-with-us/

### Does the law make a distinction between spraying Naira notes and the broader category of "abuse" or "defacement" of the national currency?
Answer: The law does not make a sharp distinction between spraying Naira notes and the broader category of abuse or defacement; spraying is treated as a specific example within the general framework of currency misuse. The Central Bank of Nigeria groups acts such as hawking, writing on the naira, and spraying together as forms of 'bastardising the national currency,' all subject to the same penalty of six months in jail or a fine of ₦50,000.

Source URL: https://dailypost.ng/2018/10/05/will-happen-nigerians-spraying-naira-notes-parties-cbn/

### Are there different penalties or applications of the law for individuals versus corporate entities (e.g., event planners) who facilitate the spraying of Naira notes?
Answer: The law does not specify different penalties or applications for individuals versus corporate entities who facilitate the spraying of Naira notes, and the general penalty for spraying Naira notes is a jail term, or a N50,000 fine or both.

Source URL: https://thecrestng.com/2019/06/16/spray-naira-notes-at-parties-and-go-to-jail-central-bank-warns-again/

### Have Nigerian authorities, such as the Central Bank of Nigeria (CBN) or law enforcement agencies, conducted public awareness campaigns or issued warnings about the illegality of spraying Naira notes?
Answer: Nigerian authorities, including the Central Bank of Nigeria (CBN), have conducted public awareness campaigns and issued warnings about the illegality of spraying Naira notes.

Source URL: https://thecrestng.com/2019/06/16/spray-naira-notes-at-parties-and-go-to-jail-central-bank-warns-again/

Answer: There is no known recent amendment, repeal, or judicial review of the law concerning the spraying of Naira notes in Nigeria.

Source URL: https://www.nigerianeye.com/2018/07/spray-naira-notes-go-to-jail-cbn-to.html

# Claim 446 — US President Trump made vaccines voluntary, not mandatory, in the US.

## Merged Q&A

Each question below was answered twice: once from the retrieved document store, and once by a reasoner working from memory alone, with no access to any document. Where the two agreed, their answers are combined into one. Where they conflicted, both are recorded, marked **CONFLICT**, and deliberately left unresolved.

**A CONFLICT means at least one of the two is wrong.** Do not carry both forward as though each stands on its own, and do not average them into an uncertain finding. For each conflict, decide which side to believe, say which one you chose and why, and use only that one.

### Did President Donald Trump issue an executive order, presidential memorandum, or sign into law a bill that made all vaccines voluntary and prohibited vaccine mandates in the United States?
Answer: There is no documented evidence that President Donald Trump issued an executive order, presidential memorandum, or signed a bill that made all vaccines voluntary or prohibited vaccine mandates in the United States.

Source URL: https://jamanetwork.com/journals/jama/fullarticle/2614172

### Did any federal agency under the Trump administration, such as the Department of Health and Human Services (HHS) or the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), issue a binding regulation or directive that changed the legal status of vaccines from mandatory to voluntary for the general U.S. population?
Answer: No federal agency under the Trump administration issued a binding regulation or directive that changed the legal status of vaccines from mandatory to voluntary for the general U.S. population. Vaccine mandates in the U.S. are primarily set at the state level; federal agencies like the CDC issue recommendations but not binding mandates for the general public. Federal authority over public health is limited to measures such as quarantine and isolation, and federal regulations do not include any mandatory vaccination programs.

Source URL: https://sgp.fas.org/crs/misc/RS21414.pdf

### Was there a specific policy enacted by the Trump administration that ended or prohibited the enforcement of the Centers for Medicare & Medicaid Services (CMS) vaccine mandate for healthcare workers?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document store returned no document bearing on whether the Trump administration enacted a policy ending or prohibiting enforcement of the CMS vaccine mandate for healthcare workers. The reasoner working from memory alone also has no recollection of any such policy and, further, reports with near-certainty that the CMS vaccine mandate was introduced under President Biden in 2021, meaning it did not exist during Trump's presidency, making any Trump-era policy to end it impossible; this conclusion is described as an inference based on adjacent facts the reasoner recalls with high confidence.

Source URL: none -- retrieval returned no document

### Did the Trump administration take formal action to remove the federal requirement for vaccinations for school enrollment, which is primarily mandated at the state level?
Answer: The Trump administration did not take formal action to remove a federal requirement for vaccinations for school enrollment because such requirements are mandated at the state level, not the federal level.

Source URL: https://www.cdc.gov/vaccines/imz-managers/laws/index.html

### Is there any official document, such as an executive order numbered in the Federal Register, that explicitly states the policy of making vaccines voluntary in the United States under President Trump?
Answer: There is no official document, such as an executive order, that explicitly states a policy of making vaccines voluntary in the United States under President Trump; instead, a related executive order focused on improving flu vaccine production and urging more Americans to be vaccinated.

Source URL: https://www.cidrap.umn.edu/influenza-vaccines/trump-signs-executive-order-improve-flu-vaccines

### Did President Trump have the constitutional authority to unilaterally override state-level vaccine mandates for schoolchildren or public health workers?
Answer: The President does not have the constitutional authority to unilaterally override state-level vaccine mandates for schoolchildren or public health workers. The preservation of public health is primarily the responsibility of state and local governments under their general police powers, with federal jurisdiction limited to interstate and foreign commerce; current federal regulations do not include any mandatory vaccination programs for the general public.

Source URL: https://sgp.fas.org/crs/misc/RS21414.pdf

Answer: No bill passed by Congress and signed by President Trump classified vaccines as purely voluntary for all Americans; federal actions under Trump focused on vaccine promotion and development, such as through executive orders urging vaccination and modernizing vaccine production.

Source URL: https://www.kff.org/news-summary/president-trump-signs-executive-order-to-modernize-flu-vaccines-improve-national-security-public-health/

# Claim 447 — A virus can easily pass through a mask.

## Merged Q&A

Each question below was answered twice: once from the retrieved document store, and once by a reasoner working from memory alone, with no access to any document. Where the two agreed, their answers are combined into one. Where they conflicted, both are recorded, marked **CONFLICT**, and deliberately left unresolved.

**A CONFLICT means at least one of the two is wrong.** Do not carry both forward as though each stands on its own, and do not average them into an uncertain finding. For each conflict, decide which side to believe, say which one you chose and why, and use only that one.

### What is the typical size range of respiratory viruses like SARS-CoV-2 or influenza, and how does this compare to the pore size of common mask materials like those used in surgical masks and N95 respirators?
Answer: Individual respiratory viruses such as SARS-CoV-2 and influenza are typically about 0.1 microns (80–140 nanometers) in diameter, but they are never airborne as naked particles—they travel within much larger respiratory droplets or aerosols generally exceeding 1 micron in size. N95 respirators are designed to filter at least 95% of airborne particles, including those around 0.3 microns, using mechanisms such as Brownian motion and electrostatic absorption rather than simple sieving, which makes them highly effective at capturing virus-laden droplets and aerosols. Surgical masks have larger pore structures but still primarily block the larger droplets responsible for viral transmission. The upshot is that common mask materials are effective barriers against respiratory viruses despite the small size of individual virus particles.

Source URL: https://www.usatoday.com/story/news/factcheck/2020/06/11/fact-check-n-95-filters-not-too-large-stop-covid-19-particles/5343537002/

### Is the primary mechanism of respiratory virus transmission via large respiratory droplets that masks are designed to block, or via smaller aerosols and bare viral particles?
Answer: The primary mechanism of respiratory virus transmission is through large respiratory droplets that masks are designed to block; airborne transmission via smaller aerosols is considered possible only in specific settings, such as during aerosol-generating procedures in medical contexts.

Source URL: https://www.who.int/news-room/commentaries/detail/modes-of-transmission-of-virus-causing-covid-19-implications-for-ipc-precaution-recommendations

### According to peer-reviewed studies, what is the particle filtration efficiency (PFE) and bacterial filtration efficiency (BFE) of standard surgical masks against viruses of varying sizes?
Answer: Standard surgical masks allow substantial penetration of virus-sized particles. A peer-reviewed study found filter penetration exceeding 5% for 100 nm particles at various breathing flows, with total inward leakage of approximately 20–25% at 85 L/min for particles in the 20–500 nm range (encompassing most viruses), leading the authors to conclude the mask may not provide substantial protection against aerosol particles up to about 500 nm. Note that PFE and BFE, by definition, do not measure viral filtration—BFE uses bacteria (~3 μm) and PFE uses inert test particles at set sizes—so peer-reviewed studies typically report PFE and BFE rather than viral filtration efficiency directly. ASTM-rated surgical masks generally achieve BFE and PFE of ≥ 95–98% at 0.1 μm depending on mask level, but independent filtration studies suggest capture of roughly 50–80% of particles in the 0.02–0.1 μm range, varying by study. In summary, masks capture a substantial but incomplete fraction of virus-sized particles, and may not reliably provide high-level protection against the smallest aerosol particles.

Source URL: https://stacks.cdc.gov/view/cdc/86382/cdc_86382_DS1.pdf

### What are the design and testing standards (e.g., NIOSH for N95, EN 149 for FFP2) for respirator masks, and do these standards include testing for filtration of particles in the size range of viruses?
Answer: Respirator masks such as N95, certified under NIOSH standards, require a filtration efficiency of at least 95% against non-oily particles in the most penetrating size range, approximately 0.3 micrometers, while the European standard EN 149 for FFP2 respirators requires a minimum of 94% filtration efficiency against similar test aerosols; these standards test particles in a size range critical for filtering airborne droplets and aerosols that can contain viruses, even though viruses themselves are not directly tested.

Source URL: https://multimedia.3m.com/mws/media/1839703O/surgical-n95-vs-standard-n95-which-to-consider.pdf

### What do major public health organizations (e.g., WHO, CDC, ECDC) state regarding the effectiveness of different mask types in reducing the transmission of viruses like influenza and SARS-CoV-2?
Answer: Major public health organizations, including the WHO, CDC, and ECDC, recommend wearing masks—including surgical masks, N95 respirators, and cloth masks—to reduce the transmission of viruses like influenza and SARS-CoV-2. The WHO's revised guidelines state that when all parties in a gathering are wearing well-constructed, well-fitting masks, it provides an extra layer of safety, as masks trap larger respiratory droplets that carry viral particles, and masks are most effective when used properly as part of broader infection control measures.

Source URL: https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2020/06/stanford-scientists-contribute-to-who-mask-guidelines.html

### Are there specific peer-reviewed laboratory studies that have measured the passage of intact, infectious viral particles through the filter media of common mask types under controlled conditions?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that peer-reviewed laboratory studies have measured the passage of intact, infectious viral particles through the filter media of common mask types under controlled conditions, citing Zhou et al. (2018) and Ueki et al. (2020) as examples. The reasoner working from memory alone reports no record of any such studies and is not confident in this assessment, being close to a guess.

Source URL: https://www.mdpi.com/2073-4360/13/12/1998

### In epidemiological studies, have populations with high rates of mask usage shown significantly lower rates of viral infection transmission compared to control groups?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document, citing a meta-analysis of 216 studies across 16 countries, states that those who used face masks had a highly significant 85% reduction in the risk of viral transmission compared to those who did not. The reasoner working from memory alone, which is fairly certain of its assessment, reports that the epidemiological evidence is mixed: some studies showed modest reductions in viral transmission associated with mask usage — for example, a large cluster-randomized trial in Bangladesh found roughly a 9-11% reduction in COVID-19 seroprevalence in communities where surgical masks were promoted — while other rigorous randomized controlled trials, such as the DANMASK-19 trial in Denmark, did not find a statistically significant reduction in infection among mask wearers compared to non-wearers, and earlier community RCTs on influenza also yielded inconsistent results; overall, the reasoner concludes the body of evidence does not uniformly demonstrate that high mask-usage populations have significantly lower viral transmission rates compared to control groups.

Source URL: https://www.pnwu.edu/blog/2020/06/23/fact-vs-fiction-face-masks/

### Does the claim hold true for "bare" viruses in aerosol form, considering that masks primarily work by stopping the larger respiratory droplets that carry the vast majority of virus particles?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document indicates that the claim holds true for bare viruses in aerosol form, stating that viruses in the 20–500 nm size range can penetrate masks, especially surgical masks, which "may not be able to provide substantial protection against these particles at any relevant combination of the breathing frequency and flow rate." The reasoner working from memory alone, recalling that masks are effective at filtering respiratory particles including smaller aerosols and that N95 masks offer high efficiency while surgical masks provide significant reduction, infers that viruses cannot easily pass through masks even in aerosol form and that masks substantially lower transmission risk. The reasoner is moderately confident in this assessment but not highly certain.

Source URL: https://stacks.cdc.gov/view/cdc/86382/cdc_86382_DS1.pdf

### Is there a scientific consensus that while no mask offers 100% filtration, properly fitted respirator masks (e.g., N95) can filter at least 95% of airborne particles, including those carrying viruses?
Answer: Properly fitted respirator masks, such as N95 masks, can filter at least 95% of airborne particles, including virus-carrying particles, though no mask offers complete filtration.

Source URL: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/9487666/

### Does the ease of a virus passing through a mask depend more on mask fit (gaps around the face) than on filtration efficiency of the mask material itself for typical respiratory viruses?
Answer: For typical respiratory viruses, the ease of a virus passing through a mask depends more on mask fit (gaps around the face) than on the filtration efficiency of the mask material itself. Studies show that even masks with potentially high filtration material can have very low Fitted Filtration Efficiency due to poor fit; for example, procedure masks secured with elastic ear loops had an FFE as low as 38.1% overall, dropping to 21.2% during head movements due to visible gaps between the mask and the wearer's face, with performance variability largely dependent on the tightness of contact between the material and the skin.

Source URL: https://jamanetwork.com/journals/jamainternalmedicine/fullarticle/2769443

# Claim 453 — US President Trump instructed his administration to halt funding of the World Health Organization while a review was conducted to assess the World Health Organization’s role regarding the spread of coronavirus.

## Merged Q&A

Each question below was answered twice: once from the retrieved document store, and once by a reasoner working from memory alone, with no access to any document. Where the two agreed, their answers are combined into one. Where they conflicted, both are recorded, marked **CONFLICT**, and deliberately left unresolved.

**A CONFLICT means at least one of the two is wrong.** Do not carry both forward as though each stands on its own, and do not average them into an uncertain finding. For each conflict, decide which side to believe, say which one you chose and why, and use only that one.

### Did President Donald Trump publicly issue an official instruction or order to his administration to halt funding for the World Health Organization?
Answer: President Donald Trump publicly issued an official instruction to halt funding for the World Health Organization, stating at a White House press conference, 'Today I'm instructing my administration to halt funding of the World Health Organization while a review is conducted.'

Source URL: https://www.cnbc.com/2020/04/14/trump-calls-for-halt-to-us-funding-for-world-health-organization-amid-coronavirus-outbreak.html

### On what specific date or during what specific time frame did President Donald Trump announce or implement the halt of funding to the World Health Organization?
Answer: President Donald Trump announced the halt of funding to the World Health Organization on April 14, 2020.

Source URL: https://www.bmj.com/content/369/bmj.m1502

### Did the United States Congress appropriate funds for the World Health Organization that were affected by this halt, and does the U.S. President possess the legal authority to unilaterally freeze or redirect such congressionally appropriated funds?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that Congress appropriated nearly $123 million for the World Health Organization in fiscal year 2020 and the Trump administration proposed cutting this amount by half, but it does not provide information on whether these funds were affected by the halt or on the President's legal authority to freeze or redirect them. The reasoner, working from memory alone and highly confident in its assessment, reports that Congress did appropriate funds that were affected by the halt, and that the President's ability to unilaterally freeze such funds is constrained by the Impoundment Control Act of 1974, which requires congressional notification and approval, with many legal authorities arguing the halt potentially violated this framework and that the President lacked the authority to freeze the funds.

Source URL: https://foreignpolicy.com/2020/02/10/trump-world-health-organization-funding-coronavirus-state-department-usaid-budget-cuts/

### Which specific U.S. funding streams to the World Health Organization (e.g., assessed membership dues, voluntary contributions) were halted as a result of President Trump's instruction?
Answer: As a result of President Trump's instruction, the United States halted its assessed membership dues and voluntary contributions to the World Health Organization.

Source URL: https://www.thenewhumanitarian.org/maps-and-graphics/2020/04/30/world-health-organisation-funding

### What was the total monetary value of the U.S. funding to the World Health Organization that was halted or withheld?
Answer: The total monetary value of the U.S. funding to the World Health Organization that was halted was approximately $400 million annually.

Source URL: https://www.bmj.com/content/369/bmj.m1502

### Did the World Health Organization issue an official public statement in response to the United States halting its funding?
Answer: **CONFLICT.** The retrieved document states that there is no indication the WHO issued an official public statement in response to the US halting funding, noting that the WHO did not respond to repeated requests for comment. In contrast, the reasoner, which is certain of its account, reports that WHO Director-General Tedros Adhanom Ghebreyesus issued a public response at a briefing and that the WHO issued a formal statement assessing the impact of the funding cut.

Source URL: https://news.wttw.com/2020/04/15/trump-s-halting-funds-who-sparks-worldwide-rebuke
